```
# S2-011 / S2-012 — Answers from Review Evidence

**Stories:** [#62](https://github.com/aum-2004/CS490_Review_Lens_AI_Fantastic_Six/issues/62) S2-011,
[#63](https://github.com/aum-2004/CS490_Review_Lens_AI_Fantastic_Six/issues/63) S2-012 |
**Rules:** S2-BR-008 through S2-BR-015 | **Builds on:** S2-013 (PR #76), S2-010 (PR #78)

## Pipeline

`answerQuestion(targetId, question, { buildReviewDatasetContext, callModel })`
(`src/services/qa/answer-question.ts`):

1. **Context (S2-010):** `buildReviewDatasetContext(targetId)` loads the signed-in owner's reviews
   for the active target, or throws.
2. **Request (S2-013):** `buildQaModelRequest` builds the system instructions, the review dataset,
   and the question, and checks the request-size budget, or throws `QaPromptError`.
3. **Model call (S2-011):** `callModel(request)`. In production this is `createOpenAiQaModel()`;
   tests pass a deterministic fake.
4. **Reply validation (S2-011 / S2-012):** `parseQaModelReply` checks the reply, or throws
   `QaResponseError`.

A failure at any step throws, and nothing unvalidated is returned. Steps 1 and 2 run before any
model call.

| Error                                       | From          | Meaning                                            |
| ------------------------------------------- | ------------- | -------------------------------------------------- |
| `ReviewContextError`, `TargetNotFoundError` | S2-010        | Not signed in, foreign or missing target, no data  |
| `QaPromptError`                             | S2-013        | Empty question, mismatched data, request too large |
| `QaModelError`                              | OpenAI client | Disabled, not configured, provider failure         |
| `QaResponseError`                           | Validation    | Malformed or ungrounded reply                      |

Every error message is user-safe. None includes review text, model output, provider bodies, or
the API key.

## Reply validation (`qa-response.ts`)

The model must reply with exactly `{"outcome", "answer", "citedReviewIds"}`. Bad replies are
rejected, not repaired:

- `INVALID_MODEL_RESPONSE`: not JSON, an unknown outcome, missing or extra fields, an empty answer,
  an answer over `MAX_ANSWER_CHARACTERS` (8,000), or non-string citations.
- `UNGROUNDED_ANSWER`:
  - a cited review ID that was not in the request (invented, or from another target); or
  - `outcome: "answered"` with no citations. S2-011 requires answers that are defensibly
    connected to the reviews, so an uncited "answer" is treated as a general-knowledge guess.

Accepted replies are normalized: the answer is trimmed, and duplicate citations are removed.
Decline outcomes never carry citations. `insufficient_evidence` may cite reviews for the part of
the question they do answer (prompt evidence rule 4).

## S2-012: insufficient evidence
### Configuration

| Variable          | Required              | Notes                                                   |
| ----------------- | --------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------- |
| `QA_ENABLED`      | Yes, exactly `"true"` | Any other value disables Q&A before any network request |
| `OPENAI_API_KEY`  | When enabled          | Server-only. Never `NEXT_PUBLIC_`.                      |
| `OPENAI_QA_MODEL` | When enabled          | Team decision. There is deliberately no default.        |

Configuration is read on every call, so a missing key or a disabled flag fails closed.

### API verification

Checked against the official OpenAI API reference and structured-outputs guide on 2026-11-12.

- **Confirmed:** endpoint, method, and auth header; `instructions` as a system/developer message;
  `input` as a string; `max_output_tokens` (minimum 16); `store`; the
  `text.format` json_schema nesting; the message content shapes `output_text` / `refusal`.
- **Not confirmed from the page:** the full list of top-level `status` values, the `usage` field
  names, and whether `output_text` appears in the raw REST body. The client is written so that
  each of these fails closed: only `completed` is accepted, usage is optional, and text is read
  from `output[]`. Confirm them with one manual call before enabling Q&A.

## Not in this change

- **Q&A Route Handler and UI (S2-009):** the route must use `requireApiUser()`, apply the
  question length limit, pass S2-010's `buildReviewDatasetContext` and `createOpenAiQaModel()`
  into `answerQuestion`, and map each error class to a response.
- **S2-010 wiring:** `buildReviewDatasetContext` is injected because PR #78 is not merged yet.
- **Live evaluation:** the S2-003 harness and the S2-004 evaluation set measure real model
  behavior. Q&A stays disabled (`QA_ENABLED`) until that run and team review.
- **Model choice:** `OPENAI_QA_MODEL` is a team decision.
```

A route should not call `runGroundedQaRequest()` directly, because that would skip reply validation.\n\nProduction wiring (after #78 merges):\n\n```ts\nanswerQuestion(targetId, question, {\n  buildReviewDatasetContext, // S2-010\n  callModel: createOpenAiQaModel(), // reads QA_ENABLED / OPENAI_API_KEY / OPENAI_QA_MODEL\n});\n```\n\n## Running it with synthetic data (test environment)\n\nNo database, network, or API key is needed:\n\n```ts\nconst context = {\n  target: { id: \"t1\", name: \"Synthetic Cafe\", platform: \"google_maps\" as const },\n  reviews: [{ id: \"r1\", analysisTargetId: \"t1\", rating: 2, text: \"Slow service.\", reviewDate: null }],\n};\n\nawait answerQuestion(\"t1\", \"Any complaints?\", {\n  buildReviewDatasetContext: async () => context,\n  // A fake model...\n  callModel: async () => ({\n    text: JSON.stringify({ outcome: \"answered\", answer: \"Slow service.\", citedReviewIds: [\"r1\"] }),\n  }),\n  // ...or the real adapter with a fake fetch, to inspect the outgoing request:\n  // callModel: createOpenAiQaModel({ fetcher: fakeFetch, env: { QA_ENABLED: \"true\", OPENAI_API_KEY: \"test\", OPENAI_QA_MODEL: \"test-model\" } }),\n});\n```\n\nRun the suites with `npx jest tests/unit/services/qa`. The controlled live smoke test (real key, synthetic data) is a separate manual step after the team approves the integration contract and spending limit.\n\n## S2-011: reply validation\n\nReplies are rejected, not repaired:\n\n| Outcome | No citations | Valid citations | Unknown citation IDs |\n| --- | --- | --- | --- |\n| `answered` | `UNGROUNDED_ANSWER` | Accepted | `UNGROUNDED_ANSWER` |\n| `insufficient_evidence` | Accepted | `UNEXPECTED_CITATIONS` | `UNGROUNDED_ANSWER` |\n| `declined_*` (all three) | Accepted | `UNEXPECTED_CITATIONS` | `UNGROUNDED_ANSWER` |\n\nMalformed replies (not JSON, unknown outcome, missing or extra fields, empty or over-8,000-character answer) are `INVALID_MODEL_RESPONSE`. Citations on declines and `insufficient_evidence` are **rejected, not silently dropped** (review follow-up). The prompt (`s2-013.v3`) states the same contract: a partial answer uses `answered` with citations and says what the reviews do not cover; every other outcome must have an empty `citedReviewIds`.\n\n## S2-012: insufficient evidence\n\nThe tests use a controlled dataset (food and service reviews only) and the issue's question, \"Do customers think the parking lot is safe at night?\":\n- the dataset sent to the model contains no parking evidence;\n- an `insufficient_evidence` reply with no citations passes through with its limitation stated;\n- an `answered` reply with no supporting reviews is rejected.\n\n## Known limitations\n\n- **Citation support is not verified.** The validator confirms each cited review ID belongs to the active dataset, but not that the review supports the claim. For example, a parking-safety claim citing a food review passes (a test documents this). Semantic support is covered by the **S2-004 evaluations**, which should include unsupported-citation cases.\n- **Fake-model tests.** These tests check the pipeline, not live model judgment. Live behavior needs the S2-003 harness and the S2-004 evaluation set.\n- **Token estimate.** Request size is estimated from UTF-8 bytes (3 per token). The smoke test should compare it with the provider-reported `usage`.\n\n## Request budget and provider gating\n\n- **Budget:** the 16,000-token application cap (`QA_MAX_REQUEST_TOKENS`) and 1,500-token output reservation (`QA_RESERVED_OUTPUT_TOKENS`) from #76 are unchanged. The full request (prompt + evidence + question + reserved output) is checked **before** the provider call. An end-to-end test with the real OpenAI adapter shows an oversized request never reaches the provider.\n- **Disabled by default:** `QA_ENABLED=false` in `.env.example`. Tests confirm the provider (injected and global `fetch`) is never called when `QA_ENABLED` is `false` or unset, and that `.env.example` ships disabled.\n- **Model:** read only from `OPENAI_QA_MODEL`, with **no fallback**. If it is unset, the call fails with `MODEL_NOT_CONFIGURED` before any network request; `QaModelError.detail` says \"OPENAI_QA_MODEL is not set. There is no default model; set it explicitly.\" for server logs. A missing key is `API_KEY_NOT_CONFIGURED`.\n\n## Security notes (for the §7 security review: third-party call and AI data boundary)\n\n- **Off by default:** no network request unless `QA_ENABLED` is exactly `\"true\"` and both `OPENAI_API_KEY` and `OPENAI_QA_MODEL` are set. Configuration is read on every call.\n- **Provider-input isolation:** the system prompt is sent only as `instructions`; review text and the question only as `input` (user message). Tested.\n- **No retention requested:** `store: false`, because the request contains customer review text.\n- **Bounded:** `max_output_tokens` from the budget, a 30 s timeout (also covers reading the body), and a 256 KiB response cap.\n- **Fail closed:** only `status: \"completed\"` is accepted; `incomplete`, `refusal`, malformed bodies, and HTTP errors map to fixed codes. No user-facing error message contains provider bodies, model output, review text, or the API key (tested with marker values).\n- **Server only:** all modules import `server-only`. No new dependencies; the API is called with `fetch`.\n\n## API verification\n\nChecked against the official OpenAI API reference and structured-outputs guide. Confirmed: endpoint, method, auth header, `instructions`, string `input`, `max_output_tokens`, `store`, `text.format` json_schema nesting, and the `output_text` / `refusal` content shapes. Per Shawn's review, the response text and `usage` fields are also documented in the [official reference](https://developers.openai.com/api/reference/resources/responses/methods/create). 1. **Citations on non-answers.** If an `insufficient_evidence` or decline reply cites real review IDs, it's now rejected (`UNEXPECTED_CITATIONS`) instead of the citations being silently dropped. Citing an unknown ID is rejected for every outcome. There are tests for all four non-answer outcomes, each with no citations, valid citations, and invalid citations.
   - I also changed the prompt. It used to tell the model to put partial answers under `insufficient_evidence` with citations, which the new rule would always reject. Partial answers now use `answered` with citations, and every other outcome must have empty citations. The prompt version is now `s2-013.v3`.
2. **Answers with no citations.** These are still rejected. I added a code comment and a "Known limitations" section to the PR: the check confirms cited IDs belong to the dataset, not that the review supports the claim. A test shows a parking claim citing a food review gets through. S2-004 covers that kind of check.
3. **Model setting.** The model comes only from `OPENAI_QA_MODEL`, with no fallback. A missing model and a missing key now get separate error codes. Each error also carries a log-only note, such as "OPENAI_QA_MODEL is not set. There is no default model…". Users still see a generic message.
4. **Request budget.** The limits are unchanged: a 16,000-token cap with 1,500 reserved for output. A new test sends an oversized request through the real OpenAI adapter and shows it never reaches the provider.

> 5. **Off by default:** `QA_ENABLED=false` stays the default. Tests confirm the provider is never called when Q&A is disabled.

The request may be sent only if\n * counted input + QA_RESERVED_OUTPUT_TOKENS <= QA_MAX_REQUEST_TOKENS (16,000 by default).\n *\n * Tokens are counted with gpt-tokenizer's `o200k_base`, the encoding its documentation lists for\n * the GPT-5, GPT-4.1, GPT-4o, and o-series model families. Any other model fails safely: no\n * provider call is made until its encoding is confirmed and added to QA_MODEL_ENCODINGS.\n */\n\n/** Model families whose tokenizer is `o200k_base` (gpt-tokenizer 4.1.0 documentation). */\nexport const QA_MODEL_ENCODINGS: ReadonlyArray<{ pattern: RegExp; encoding: \"o200k_base\" }> = [\n  { pattern: /^gpt-5(?:[.-]|$)/, encoding: \"o200k_base\" },\n  { pattern: /^gpt-4\\.1(?:-|$)/, encoding: \"o200k_base\" },\n  { pattern: /^gpt-4o(?:-|$)/, encoding: \"o200k_base\" },\n  { pattern: /^o[134](?:-|$)/, encoding: \"o200k_base\" },\n];\n\n/**\n * Allowance for tokens the provider adds that are not in the request text: message framing\n * (role and separator tokens for the system and user messages, and reply priming, a few tokens\n * each) and the formatting the provider wraps around the structured-output schema. The schema\n * JSON itself is counted exactly. OpenAI does not publish the exact framing, so this is a\n * deliberately generous allowance; check it against `usage.input_tokens` in the smoke test.\n */\nexport const QA_MODEL_OVERHEAD_TOKENS = 256;\n\n/** Review text is untrusted: count special-token strings like \"<|endoftext|>\" as plain text. */\nconst COUNT_OPTIONS = { disallowedSpecial: new Set<string>() };\n\nexport class QaBudgetError extends Error {\n  /** @param detail Operator-facing explanation for server logs; never shown to users. */\n  constructor(\n    readonly code: \"MODEL_BUDGET_UNSUPPORTED\" | \"BUDGET_CHECK_FAILED\",\n    readonly detail: string,\n  ) {\n    super(\"Review Q&A is not available for this configuration.\");\n    this.name = \"QaBudgetError\";\n  }\n}\n\nexport function resolveTokenEncoding(model: string): \"o200k_base\" {\n  const match = QA_MODEL_ENCODINGS.find(({ pattern }) => pattern.test(model));\n  if (!match) {\n    throw new QaBudgetError(\n      \"MODEL_BUDGET_UNSUPPORTED\",\n      `No token encoding is registered for OPENAI_QA_MODEL=\"${model}\". Confirm its tokenizer and ` +\n        \"add it to QA_MODEL_ENCODINGS before enabling Q&A with this model.\",\n    );\n  }\n  return match.encoding;\n}\n\n/** Every piece of text the provider turns into input tokens. */\nexport interface ModelInputParts {\n  instructions: string;\n  input: string;\n  /** The exact structured-output format object sent to the provider. */\n  responseFormat: unknown;\n}\n\nexport interface QaTokenBudget {\n  maxRequestTokens: number;\n  reservedOutputTokens: number;\n}\n\nexport const QA_TOKEN_BUDGET: QaTokenBudget = {\n  maxRequestTokens: QA_MAX_REQUEST_TOKENS,\n  reservedOutputTokens: QA_RESERVED_OUTPUT_TOKENS,\n};\n\n/**\n * Counts the model input against the budget. Returns the counted input tokens, or throws:\n * QaPromptError(\"REQUEST_TOO_LARGE\") when over budget, QaBudgetError when the model is unsupported\n * or counting fails. Counting stops early once a part exceeds what remains, so an oversized\n * question is never fully tokenized.\n */\nexport function enforceModelInputBudget(\n  parts: ModelInputParts,\n  model: string,\n  budget: QaTokenBudget = QA_TOKEN_BUDGET,\n): number {\n  resolveTokenEncoding(model);\n\n  const texts = [parts.instructions, parts.input, JSON.stringify(parts.responseFormat)];\n  let remaining = budget.maxRequestTokens - budget.reservedOutputTokens - QA_MODEL_OVERHEAD_TOKENS;\n  let counted = QA_MODEL_OVERHEAD_TOKENS;\n\n  for (const text of texts) {\n    let tokens: number | false;\n    try {\n      tokens = remaining < 0 ? false : isWithinTokenLimit(text, remaining, COUNT_OPTIONS);\n    } catch {\n      throw new QaBudgetError(\n        \"BUDGET_CHECK_FAILED\",\n        \"The request could not be tokenized; it was not sent.\",\n      );\n    }\n    if (tokens === false) {\n      throw new QaPromptError(\n        \"REQUEST_TOO_LARGE\",\n        \"This question is too long to analyze with these reviews. Try a shorter question.\",\n      );\n    }\n    remaining -= tokens;\n    counted += tokens;\n  }\n  return counted;\n}\n"
```json
}
```

```
  promptVersion: string;
  targetId: string;

  instructions: string;

  input: string;

  reviewIds: string[];
  /** Estimated tokens for `instructions` + `input`. */

  /** Output limit to send to the provider (QA_RESERVED_OUTPUT_TOKENS). */
  maxOutputTokens: number;
}
```

```
export function createOpenAiQaModel({
  fetcher,
  env,
  budget,
}: OpenAiQaModelOptions = {}): QaModelCaller {
  return async (request) => {
    const { apiKey, model } = readOpenAiQaConfig(env);
    const body = buildOpenAiRequestBody(request, model);
    // Full-request budget, counted on exactly what is sent. Throws before any network request.
    const inputTokens = enforceModelInputBudget(
      { instructions: body.instructions, input: body.input, responseFormat: body.text.format },
      model,
      budget,
    );

    let response: Response;
    try {
      response = await (fetcher ?? fetch)(OPENAI_RESPONSES_URL, {
        method: "POST",
        headers: {
          Authorization: `Bearer ${apiKey}`,
          "Content-Type": "application/json",
          Accept: "application/json",
        },
        body: JSON.stringify(body),
        cache: "no-store",
        signal: AbortSignal.timeout(OPENAI_TIMEOUT_MS),
      });
    } catch (error) {
      throw new QaModelError(isTimeoutError(error) ? "MODEL_TIMEOUT" : "MODEL_UNAVAILABLE");
    }

    if (!response.ok) {
      await response.body?.cancel().catch(() => {});
      throw errorForStatus(response.status);
    }
    return { ...extractReply(await readBoundedJson(response)), countedInputTokens: inputTokens };
  };
}
```

```
describe("request size budget", () => {
  it("estimates tokens from UTF-8 bytes", () => {
    expect(estimateTokens("")).toBe(0);
    expect(estimateTokens("abc")).toBe(1);
    expect(estimateTokens("abcd")).toBe(2);
    // Multi-byte characters count by bytes, not characters.
    expect(estimateTokens("é".repeat(3))).toBe(Math.ceil(6 / ESTIMATED_BYTES_PER_TOKEN));
  });

  it("reports the estimated input size and reserved output on every request", () => {
    const request = buildRequest();

    expect(request.estimatedInputTokens).toBe(
      estimateTokens(request.instructions) + estimateTokens(request.input),
    );
    expect(request.maxOutputTokens).toBe(QA_RESERVED_OUTPUT_TOKENS);
  });

  it("counts the prompt template, evidence, question, and reserved output together", () => {
    const base = buildRequest({ question: "q" });
    // Leave exactly enough room for the remaining question bytes, then go one token over.
    const spareTokens =
      QA_MAX_REQUEST_TOKENS - QA_RESERVED_OUTPUT_TOKENS - base.estimatedInputTokens;
    const fits = "q".repeat(1 + spareTokens * ESTIMATED_BYTES_PER_TOKEN - 2);
    const tooBig = "q".repeat(1 + (spareTokens + 1) * ESTIMATED_BYTES_PER_TOKEN);

    const accepted = buildRequest({ question: fits });
    expect(accepted.estimatedInputTokens + QA_RESERVED_OUTPUT_TOKENS).toBeLessThanOrEqual(
      QA_MAX_REQUEST_TOKENS,
    );
    expect(() => buildRequest({ question: tooBig })).toThrow(
      expect.objectContaining({ name: "QaPromptError", code: "REQUEST_TOO_LARGE" }),
    );
  });

  it("rejects oversized evidence even when the question is short", () => {
    const bulky = Array.from({ length: 20 }, (_, i) => ({
      ...reviews[0],
      id: `bulk-${i}`,
      text: "x".repeat(QA_MAX_REQUEST_TOKENS),
    }));

    let thrown: unknown;
    try {
      buildRequest({ reviews: bulky });
    } catch (error) {
      thrown = error;
    }
    expect(thrown).toBeInstanceOf(QaPromptError);
    expect(thrown).toMatchObject({ code: "REQUEST_TOO_LARGE" });
    expect((thrown as Error).message).not.toContain("xxxx");
  });
});

  type PromptReview,
  type PromptTarget,
  type QaDeclineOutcome,
} from "@/services/qa/system-prompt";

jest.mock("server-only", () => ({}));
```

```
      const { deps } = setup(defaultContext);
      const hugeQuestion = "Is the food good? ".repeat(QA_MAX_REQUEST_TOKENS);

      expect(error).toMatchObject({ name: "QaPromptError", code: "REQUEST_TOO_LARGE" });
      expect(deps.invokeModel).not.toHaveBeenCalled();

  it("fits S2-010's largest allowed evidence plus a 2,000-character question in the budget", async () => {
    // Grow review text until the S2-010 evidence measurement is as close to its cap as possible.
    const contextWithTextLength = (length: number) =>
      makeContext(
        Array.from({ length: S2010_MAX_CONTEXT_REVIEWS }, (_, i) =>
          review(i, "The service was friendly. ".repeat(50).slice(0, length)),
        ),
      );
    let textLength = 1;
    while (
      contextWithTextLength(textLength + 1).evidenceBytes <= S2010_MAX_CONTEXT_EVIDENCE_BYTES
    ) {
      textLength += 1;
    }
    const maxContext = contextWithTextLength(textLength);
    expect(maxContext.evidenceBytes).toBeLessThanOrEqual(S2010_MAX_CONTEXT_EVIDENCE_BYTES);
    expect(maxContext.evidenceBytes).toBeGreaterThan(S2010_MAX_CONTEXT_EVIDENCE_BYTES - 200);
    const { deps } = setup(maxContext);

    await runGroundedQaRequest(TARGET_ID, "q".repeat(2_000), deps);

    const request = deps.invokeModel.mock.calls[0][0];
    expect(request.estimatedInputTokens + request.maxOutputTokens).toBeLessThanOrEqual(
      QA_MAX_REQUEST_TOKENS,
    );
  });
});
```

```
- `REQUEST_TOO_LARGE`: the estimated system prompt + review evidence + question, plus
  `QA_RESERVED_OUTPUT_TOKENS`, exceeds `QA_MAX_REQUEST_TOKENS` (see below).

### Request size budget

| Part            | Limit                                                                       |
| --------------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| Review evidence | S2-010: at most 100 reviews and 32 KiB of evidence JSON (checked first)     |
| Whole request   | `QA_MAX_REQUEST_TOKENS` = 16,000 estimated tokens                           |
| Of which, reply | `QA_RESERVED_OUTPUT_TOKENS` = 1,500, sent to the provider as the output cap |

Tokens are estimated as UTF-8 bytes / `ESTIMATED_BYTES_PER_TOKEN` (3), with no tokenizer
dependency. A test checks that S2-010's largest allowed evidence plus a 2,000-character question
fits. These are application budgets for cost and latency, not the model's context window. Change
the budget only through `QA_MAX_REQUEST_TOKENS`.

### Wiring to S2-010

- The prompt reduces, but cannot guarantee, out-of-scope or ungrounded model output. Scope behavior
  is measured with the S2-004 evaluation set; S2-011 validates the response structure.
- The token budget caps total request size, but the route should still apply its own question
  length limit (S2-009) so users get a clear message before any database work.
- **Still to verify during S2-011 integration:**
  - Provider-input isolation: `instructions` is sent as the system message and review text and
    the question only in the user message, never concatenated into the system message.
  - Response validation: unknown outcomes, malformed JSON, and `citedReviewIds` outside the
    request's `reviewIds` are rejected before anything reaches the user.
  - Full request-size limits: the byte-based estimate matches the provider's reported input
    tokens for realistic datasets, and `maxOutputTokens` is actually sent.
- The S2-BR rule text was not available in the repository when this was written. The prompt was
- **Provider-input isolation:** `instructions` (the system prompt) is the only system-level input.
  Review text and the question are sent only in `input` (the user message). A test checks this.
- **Structured output:** `text.format` is a strict JSON schema (`QA_REPLY_JSON_SCHEMA`) that
  mirrors the validator. The validator still runs on every reply.
- **Output limit:** `max_output_tokens` is `QA_RESERVED_OUTPUT_TOKENS` from the request budget.
- **Retention:** `store: false`, because the request contains customer review text.
- **Response handling:** only `status: "completed"` is accepted. `incomplete` becomes
  `RESPONSE_INCOMPLETE`, and a `refusal` content part becomes `MODEL_REFUSED`. The text is read
  from `output[].content[]` parts of type `output_text`. Bodies are capped at 256 KiB.
  `usage.input_tokens` / `output_tokens` are returned when present, so the byte-based token
  estimate can be compared with real usage.
- **HTTP errors:** 429 → `MODEL_RATE_LIMITED`; 401 / 403 → `MODEL_AUTH`; other 4xx (except 408) →
  `MODEL_REJECTED`; 408 / 5xx / network → `MODEL_UNAVAILABLE`; timeout → `MODEL_TIMEOUT`.
```

```json
  "content": "import \"server-only\";\n\nimport {\n  QA_MAX_REQUEST_TOKENS,\n  QA_RESERVED_OUTPUT_TOKENS,\n  QaPromptError,\n} from \"@/services/qa/system-prompt\";\n\n/**\n * S2-011: request budget, enforced before the generation call.\n *\n * The authoritative input size is OpenAI's own count: POST /v1/responses/input_tokens with the\n * same model, instructions, input, and structured-output format as the generation request. Per\n * the official token-counting guide, that count \"includes formatting tokens used to represent\n * request structure, such as message roles and boundaries\", so no local overhead allowance is\n * needed. Generation is allowed only if input_tokens + QA_RESERVED_OUTPUT_TOKENS <=\n * QA_MAX_REQUEST_TOKENS (16,000 by default). Any counting failure fails closed.\n */\n\n/**\n * Hard ceiling checked before any network call, so an absurdly large request is never sent to the\n * provider, even for counting. 128 KiB is about 4× S2-010's 32 KiB evidence limit plus the prompt;\n * a request this large would need more than 9 bytes per token to fit 14,500 tokens, far above\n * real review text. Exceeding it is treated as REQUEST_TOO_LARGE (fails closed).\n */\nexport const MAX_COUNTABLE_REQUEST_BYTES = 131_072;\n\nexport interface QaTokenBudget {\n  maxRequestTokens: number;\n  reservedOutputTokens: number;\n}\n\nexport const QA_TOKEN_BUDGET: QaTokenBudget = {\n  maxRequestTokens: QA_MAX_REQUEST_TOKENS,\n  reservedOutputTokens: QA_RESERVED_OUTPUT_TOKENS,\n};\n\n/** The token count could not be trusted. The generation request is never sent. */\nexport class QaBudgetError extends Error {\n  /** @param detail Operator-facing explanation for server logs; never shown to users. */\n  constructor(\n    readonly code: \"BUDGET_CHECK_FAILED\",\n    readonly detail: string,\n  ) {\n    super(\"We couldn't check the size of this question. Please try again.\");\n    this.name = \"QaBudgetError\";\n  }\n}\n\nfunction tooLarge(): QaPromptError {\n  return new QaPromptError(\n    \"REQUEST_TOO_LARGE\",\n    \"This question is too long to analyze with these reviews. Try a shorter question.\",\n  );\n}\n\n/** Rejects a request whose serialized payload exceeds MAX_COUNTABLE_REQUEST_BYTES. */\nexport function assertCountableSize(payload: unknown): void {\n  const bytes = Buffer.byteLength(JSON.stringify(payload), \"utf8\");\n  if (bytes > MAX_COUNTABLE_REQUEST_BYTES) throw tooLarge();\n}\n\n/**\n * Reads `{\"object\": \"response.input_tokens\", \"input_tokens\": <integer>}` strictly. Anything else\n * (wrong object type, missing, negative, fractional, non-numeric, or unsafe counts) fails closed.\n */\nexport function parseInputTokenCount(body: unknown): number {\n  const count =\n    typeof body === \"object\" && body !== null && !Array.isArray(body)\n      ? (body as Record<string, unknown>)\n      : undefined;\n  if (count?.object !== \"response.input_tokens\") {\n    throw new QaBudgetError(\"BUDGET_CHECK_FAILED\", \"Token count response had an unexpected shape.\");\n  }\n  const tokens = count.input_tokens;\n  if (typeof tokens !== \"number\" || !Number.isSafeInteger(tokens) || tokens < 0) {\n    throw new QaBudgetError(\"BUDGET_CHECK_FAILED\", \"Token count response had an invalid count.\");\n  }\n  return tokens;\n}\n\n/** Throws REQUEST_TOO_LARGE unless inputTokens + reserved output fits the cap. */\nexport function enforceTokenBudget(\n  inputTokens: number,\n  budget: QaTokenBudget = QA_TOKEN_BUDGET,\n): void {\n  if (inputTokens + budget.reservedOutputTokens > budget.maxRequestTokens) throw tooLarge();\n}\n"
}
```

```
60	export interface OpenAiQaConfig {
61	  apiKey: string;
62	  model: string;

64	
65	/** Environment source. The client reads only QA_ENABLED, OPENAI_API_KEY, and OPENAI_QA_MODEL. */
66	export type QaEnv = Readonly<Record<string, string | undefined>>;
67	
68	/** Paid model calls stay off unless QA_ENABLED is exactly "true" (same rule as ingestion). */
69	export function readOpenAiQaConfig(env: QaEnv = process.env): OpenAiQaConfig {
70	  if (env.QA_ENABLED !== "true") {
71	    throw new QaModelError("QA_DISABLED", 'QA_ENABLED is not "true"; Q&A is off.');
72	  }
73	  const apiKey = [REDACTED]?.trim();
74	  if (!apiKey) throw new QaModelError("API_KEY_NOT_CONFIGURED", "OPENAI_API_KEY is not set.");
75	  // No fallback model: the model is a team decision and must be set explicitly.
76	  const model = env.OPENAI_QA_MODEL?.trim();
77	  if (!model) {
78	    throw new QaModelError(
79	      "MODEL_NOT_CONFIGURED",
80	      "OPENAI_QA_MODEL is not set. There is no default model; set it explicitly.",
81	    );
82	  }
83	  return { apiKey, model };
84	}
85	
86	export function buildOpenAiRequestBody(request: QaModelRequest, model: string) {
87	  return {
88	    model,
89	    // Provider-input isolation: trusted rules go only in `instructions` (system/developer
90	    // message); untrusted review text and the question go only in `input` (user message).
91	    instructions: request.instructions,
92	    input: request.input,
93	    max_output_tokens: request.maxOutputTokens,
94	    // Review text is customer data; do not ask the provider to retain the exchange.
95	    store: false,
96	    text: {
97	      format: {
98	        type: "json_schema",
99	        name: "reviewlens_qa_reply",
100	        schema: QA_REPLY_JSON_SCHEMA,
101	        strict: true,
102	      },
103	    },
104	  };
105	}
106	
107	function isTimeoutError(error: unknown): boolean {
108	  return (
109	    typeof error === "object" &&
110	    error !== null &&
111	    "name" in error &&
112	    (error.name === "TimeoutError" || error.name === "AbortError")
113	  );
114	}
115	
116	async function readBoundedJson(response: Response): Promise<unknown> {
117	  const declared = Number(response.headers.get("content-length"));
118	  if (declared > MAX_OPENAI_RESPONSE_BYTES || !response.body) {
119	    await response.body?.cancel().catch(() => {});
120	    throw new QaModelError("MODEL_FAILED");
121	  }
122	  const reader = response.body.getReader();
123	  const chunks: Uint8Array[] = [];
124	  let bytes = 0;
125	  try {
126	    while (true) {
127	      const { done, value } = await reader.read();
128	      if (done) break;
129	      bytes += value.byteLength;
130	      if (bytes > MAX_OPENAI_RESPONSE_BYTES) {
131	        await reader.cancel().catch(() => {});
132	        throw new QaModelError("MODEL_FAILED");
133	      }
134	      chunks.push(value);
135	    }
136	  } catch (error) {
137	    // The timeout signal also covers reading the body; never let a raw stream error escape.
138	    if (error instanceof QaModelError) throw error;
139	    throw new QaModelError(isTimeoutError(error) ? "MODEL_TIMEOUT" : "MODEL_UNAVAILABLE");
140	  } finally {
141	    reader.releaseLock();
142	  }
143	  try {
144	    return JSON.parse(Buffer.concat(chunks).toString("utf8"));
145	  } catch {
146	    throw new QaModelError("MODEL_FAILED");
147	  }
148	}
149	
150	function errorForStatus(status: number): QaModelError {
151	  if (status === 429) return new QaModelError("MODEL_RATE_LIMITED");
152	  if (status === 401 || status === 403) return new QaModelError("MODEL_AUTH");
153	  if (status >= 400 && status < 500 && status !== 408) return new QaModelError("MODEL_REJECTED");
154	  return new QaModelError("MODEL_UNAVAILABLE");
155	}
156	
157	type Json = Record<string, unknown>;
158	const isObject = (value: unknown): value is Json =>
159	  typeof value === "object" && value !== null && !Array.isArray(value);
160	
161	/** Reads the reply text from `output[].content[]`; `output_text` is treated as SDK-only. */
162	export function extractReply(body: unknown): QaModelReply {
163	  if (!isObject(body)) throw new QaModelError("MODEL_FAILED");
164	  if (body.status === "incomplete") throw new QaModelError("RESPONSE_INCOMPLETE");
165	  if (body.status !== "completed" || !Array.isArray(body.output)) {
166	    throw new QaModelError("MODEL_FAILED");
167	  }
168	
169	  const texts: string[] = [];
170	  for (const item of body.output) {
171	    if (!isObject(item) || item.type !== "message" || !Array.isArray(item.content)) continue;
172	    for (const part of item.content) {
173	      if (!isObject(part)) continue;
174	      if (part.type === "refusal") throw new QaModelError("MODEL_REFUSED");
175	      if (part.type === "output_text" && typeof part.text === "string") texts.push(part.text);
176	    }
177	  }
178	  const text = texts.join("");
179	  if (text.trim() === "") throw new QaModelError("MODEL_FAILED");
180	
181	  const usage = isObject(body.usage) ? body.usage : undefined;
182	  return typeof usage?.input_tokens === "number" && typeof usage.output_tokens === "number"
183	    ? { text, usage: { inputTokens: usage.input_tokens, outputTokens: usage.output_tokens } }
184	    : { text };
185	}
186	
187	export interface OpenAiQaModelOptions {
188	  fetcher?: typeof fetch;
189	  env?: QaEnv;
190	  /** Tests only. Production always uses QA_TOKEN_BUDGET (16,000 total, 1,500 reserved output). */
191	  budget?: QaTokenBudget;
192	}
193	
194	/**
195	 * Production QaModelCaller. Configuration is read on every call, so turning QA_ENABLED off takes
196	 * effect without a restart and a missing key fails closed before any network request.
197	 */
198	export function createOpenAiQaModel({
199	  fetcher,
200	  env,
201	  budget,
202	}: OpenAiQaModelOptions = {}): QaModelCaller {
203	  return async (request) => {
204	    const { apiKey, model } = readOpenAiQaConfig(env);
205	    const body = buildOpenAiRequestBody(request, model);
206	    // Full-request budget, counted on exactly what is sent. Throws before any network request.
207	    const inputTokens = enforceModelInputBudget(
208	      { instructions: body.instructions, input: body.input, responseFormat: body.text.format },
209	      model,
210	      budget,
211	    );
212	
213	    let response: Response;
214	    try {
215	      response = await (fetcher ?? fetch)(OPENAI_RESPONSES_URL, {
216	        method: "POST",
217	        headers: {
218	          Authorization: `Bearer ${apiKey}`,
219	          "Content-Type": "application/json",
220	          Accept: "application/json",
221	        },
222	        body: JSON.stringify(body),
223	        cache: "no-store",
224	        signal: AbortSignal.timeout(OPENAI_TIMEOUT_MS),
225	      });
226	    } catch (error) {
227	      throw new QaModelError(isTimeoutError(error) ? "MODEL_TIMEOUT" : "MODEL_UNAVAILABLE");
228	    }
229	
230	    if (!response.ok) {
231	      await response.body?.cancel().catch(() => {});
232	      throw errorForStatus(response.status);
233	    }
234	    return { ...extractReply(await readBoundedJson(response)), countedInputTokens: inputTokens };
235	  };
236	}
237
```

```
=== neel-0064 APPROVED 2026-11-13T22:01:22Z
**Independent security-focused review — APPROVED for S2-011's submitted provider/data-boundary and reply-validation scope at `8b4ca92`.** I reviewed `answer-question.ts`, `qa-request.ts` / `system-prompt.ts` as composed on this stacked branch, `openai-qa-client.ts`, `qa-response.ts`, tests, `.env.example`, documentation and the committed redacted AI session. GitHub CI #154 passed on this head. No blocking security issue found in the code under review.

**Verified:**
- Provider calls fail closed unless `QA_ENABLED === "true"`, `OPENAI_API_KEY` and explicitly configured `OPENAI_QA_MODEL` exist; no hard-coded/fallback model or client-side key. Fixed HTTPS OpenAI endpoint and no new SDK/dependencies.
- Authorized-context builder and total prompt/evidence/question budget run before `callModel`; error paths prevent the provider call. Production must supply the S2-010 authenticated/ownership-checked builder.
- Only minimal review projection (ID, rating, date, text) is composed; reviewer metadata, URLs and source metadata are excluded. Trusted prompt goes in `instructions`, dataset/question in `input`; API request uses `store:false`, `max_output_tokens`, 30-second abort, a 256 KiB response cap, and strict JSON schema. `store:false` is a storage request setting, not a guarantee of zero provider retention.
- Provider/network/status/refusal/errors are mapped to safe fixed error messages, without exposing credentials, response bodies or review text. Reply validation rejects malformed JSON, unknown/foreign citations, uncited answers, and citations on insufficient/decline outcomes rather than silently removing them. Regression tests cover the negative paths.
- The linked S2-011 Claude session is now committed and contains prompts, assistant messages, tool calls and results (not merely a summary). Spot-check/redaction-pattern scan found no obvious live API credentials; I cannot attest word-for-word to the original local session.

**Security gates not certified by this approval:**
1. This PR is **draft and stacked on #76**. Retarget after #76 merges; recheck the diff/CI and any changes to the model boundary. Shawn's outstanding CHANGES_REQUESTED functional review must be resolved by Shawn; my previous functional comments and this security approval do **not** count as two distinct human reviewers.
2. The deployed S2-009 route / #82 composition must use S2-010's *real session-verified ownership guard*, server-side question limits and safe error mapping; the injection seam here is intentionally testable, not itself an authentication check. Verify cross-user and target-switch isolation before enabling the feature.
3. The prompt contains a JSON-quoted **user-controlled target name** inside higher-priority `instructions`. Quoting preserves format, not instruction-following safety; review-text/question prompt injection and PII embedded *inside* free-text reviews also remain risks. Add adversarial tests in the S2-003/S2-004/S2-016 integration/evaluation, and consider keeping user-controlled target names entirely in lower-trust input during hardening.
4. Review IDs are membership-checked, **not semantically grounded**. Add an unsupported-claim-with-plausible-citation S2-004 test (e.g., parking claim backed only by a food review). Compare observed provider usage with estimated token budget. Require a controlled, team-approved synthetic-data live smoke test; leave `QA_ENABLED=false` until those gates pass.

**Verdict:** Security approval for this isolated S2-011 provider/pipeline implementation, **not** approval to merge this draft yet, enable Q&A, or mark #62/#63 fully Done.
```

```
import "server-only";

import {
  buildQaModelRequest,
  type PromptReview,
  type PromptTarget,
  type QaModelRequest,
} from "@/services/qa/system-prompt";

/**
 * The part of the S2-010 review context this module reads. S2-010's `ReviewDatasetContext`
 * (`buildReviewDatasetContext`, PR #78) satisfies it structurally, so S2-013 does not import the
 * unmerged module. Its extra fields (`target.sourceUrl`, `evidenceJson`, ...) are ignored.
 */
export interface QaReviewContext {
  readonly target: Readonly<PromptTarget>;
  readonly reviews: readonly Readonly<PromptReview>[];
}

/** Builds the authorized context for a target, or throws. In production: S2-010's builder. */
export type QaReviewContextBuilder = (targetId: unknown) => Promise<QaReviewContext>;

/** The single model boundary. S2-011 supplies the provider call; S2-003 supplies a mock. */
export type QaModelInvoker<T> = (request: QaModelRequest) => Promise<T>;

export interface GroundedQaDependencies<T> {
  buildReviewDatasetContext: QaReviewContextBuilder;
  invokeModel: QaModelInvoker<T>;
}

/**
 * Adapter from the S2-010 context to the S2-013 prompt input. The types already match; this
 * explicit projection guarantees only the listed fields reach the prompt builder, which encodes
 * its own dataset (S2-010's `evidenceJson` is never sent).
 */
export function toPromptContext(context: QaReviewContext): {
  target: PromptTarget;
  reviews: PromptReview[];
} {
  return {
    target: {
      id: context.target.id,
      name: context.target.name,
      platform: context.target.platform,
    },
    reviews: context.reviews.map((review) => ({
      id: review.id,
      analysisTargetId: review.analysisTargetId,
      rating: review.rating,
      text: review.text,
      reviewDate: review.reviewDate,
    })),
  };
}

/**
 * Builds the authorized review context for `targetId` (S2-010), then the model request (S2-013),
 * and only then calls the model. If either builder throws (S2-010's ReviewContextError or
 * TargetNotFoundError, or QaPromptError), the error propagates and `invokeModel` is never called.
 */
export async function runGroundedQaRequest<T>(
  targetId: unknown,
  question: string,
  { buildReviewDatasetContext, invokeModel }: GroundedQaDependencies<T>,
): Promise<T> {
  const context = await buildReviewDatasetContext(targetId);
  const request = buildQaModelRequest({ ...toPromptContext(context), question });
  return invokeModel(request);
}
import "server-only";

import { runGroundedQaRequest, type QaReviewContextBuilder } from "@/services/qa/qa-request";
import { parseQaModelReply, type QaAnswer } from "@/services/qa/qa-response";
import type { QaModelRequest } from "@/services/qa/system-prompt";

/** Raw reply from a model provider, before validation. */
export interface QaModelReply {
  /** The model's text output; expected to be the S2-013 JSON reply. */
  text: string;
  /** Provider-reported token usage, when available. */
  usage?: { inputTokens: number; outputTokens: number };
  /**
   * Input tokens reported by OpenAI's token-count endpoint before generation. It should equal
   * `usage.inputTokens`; log both to confirm in the smoke test.
   */
  countedInputTokens?: number;
}

/** Calls a model provider. Production: `createOpenAiQaModel()`. Tests: a deterministic fake. */
export type QaModelCaller = (request: QaModelRequest) => Promise<QaModelReply>;

export interface AnswerQuestionDependencies {
  /** S2-010's `buildReviewDatasetContext`. */
  buildReviewDatasetContext: QaReviewContextBuilder;
  callModel: QaModelCaller;
}

/**
 * S2-011: answers a question about the active target from its reviews.
 *
 * Order: authorized review context (S2-010) → model request (S2-013) → model call → reply
 * validation. A failure at any step throws and nothing unvalidated is returned. Errors are
 * ReviewContextError / TargetNotFoundError (S2-010), QaPromptError (request, including
 * REQUEST_TOO_LARGE from the pre-generation budget check), QaBudgetError (invalid token count),
 * QaModelError (provider, including failed token-count calls), or QaResponseError (reply).
 */
export async function answerQuestion(
  targetId: unknown,
  question: string,
  { buildReviewDatasetContext, callModel }: AnswerQuestionDependencies,
): Promise<QaAnswer> {
  return runGroundedQaRequest(targetId, question, {
    buildReviewDatasetContext,
    invokeModel: async (request) => parseQaModelReply((await callModel(request)).text, request),
  });
}
```

```
    throw new ReviewQuestionError(
      "FAILED",
      message ?? "ReviewLens couldn't answer your question. Please try again later.",
      fieldErrors,
    );
  }

  let payload: unknown;
  try {
    payload = await response.json();
  } catch {
    // Never show raw server/provider diagnostics.
  }
  if (
    typeof payload === "object" &&
    payload !== null &&
    "success" in payload &&
    payload.success === true &&
    "data" in payload
  ) {
    const data = payload.data;
    if (
      typeof data === "object" &&
      data !== null &&
      "targetId" in data &&
      data.targetId === targetId &&
      "outcome" in data &&
      ["answered", "insufficient_evidence", "out_of_scope"].includes(String(data.outcome)) &&
      "answer" in data &&
      typeof data.answer === "string" &&
      data.answer.trim()
    ) {
      return data as ReviewAnswer;
    }
  }
  throw new ReviewQuestionError(
    "FAILED",
    "ReviewLens returned an unexpected response. Please try again.",
  );
}
```

```
 * Fake OpenAI: routes the token-count and generation endpoints separately, so tests can assert
 * that the generation endpoint is (or is not) called.
 */
function provider({
  count = () => jsonResponse(countBody()),
  generation = () => jsonResponse(completed()),
}: { count?: () => Reply; generation?: () => Reply } = {}) {
  const fetcher = jest.fn<Promise<Response>, Parameters<typeof fetch>>(async (url) => {
    const reply = String(url) === OPENAI_INPUT_TOKENS_URL ? count() : generation();
    if (!(reply instanceof Response)) throw reply;
    return reply;
  });
  const callsTo = (url: string) => fetcher.mock.calls.filter(([u]) => String(u) === url);
  return {
    fetcher,
    countCalls: () => callsTo(OPENAI_INPUT_TOKENS_URL),
    generationCalls: () => callsTo(OPENAI_RESPONSES_URL),
  };
}

const bodyOf = (call: Parameters<typeof fetch>) => JSON.parse(String(call[1]?.body));

async function rejectionOf(promise: Promise<unknown>): Promise<Error & { code?: string }> {
  try {
    await promise;
  } catch (error) {
    expect((error as Error).message).not.toContain("SECRET_MARKER");
    return error as Error & { code?: string };
  }
  throw new Error("Expected a rejection.");
}

async function codeOf(promise: Promise<unknown>): Promise<QaModelErrorCode> {
  const error = await rejectionOf(promise);
  expect(error).toBeInstanceOf(QaModelError);
  return (error as QaModelError).code;
}

describe("readOpenAiQaConfig", () => {
  it.each([{}, { QA_ENABLED: "false" }, { QA_ENABLED: "TRUE" }, { QA_ENABLED: "1" }])(
    "is disabled unless QA_ENABLED is exactly true (%j)",
    (flags) => {
      expect(() => readOpenAiQaConfig({ ...env, QA_ENABLED: undefined, ...flags })).toThrow(
        expect.objectContaining({ code: "QA_DISABLED" }),
      );
    },
  );

  it.each([{ OPENAI_API_KEY: undefined }, { OPENAI_API_KEY: "  " }])(
    "requires an API key (%j)",
    (missing) => {
      expect(() => readOpenAiQaConfig({ ...env, ...missing })).toThrow(
        expect.objectContaining({
          code: "API_KEY_NOT_CONFIGURED",
          detail: "OPENAI_API_KEY is not set.",
        }),
      );
    },
  );

  it.each([{ OPENAI_QA_MODEL: undefined }, { OPENAI_QA_MODEL: "" }, { OPENAI_QA_MODEL: "  " }])(
    "requires OPENAI_QA_MODEL with no fallback model (%j)",
    (missing) => {
      let thrown: unknown;
      try {
        readOpenAiQaConfig({ ...env, ...missing });
      } catch (error) {
        thrown = error;
      }
      expect(thrown).toMatchObject({ name: "QaModelError", code: "MODEL_NOT_CONFIGURED" });
      expect((thrown as QaModelError).detail).toMatch(/OPENAI_QA_MODEL is not set.*no default/);
      // The user-facing message stays generic; the detail is for server logs only.
      expect((thrown as Error).message).toBe("Review Q&A is not configured.");
    },
  );

  it("returns the trimmed configuration", () => {
    expect(readOpenAiQaConfig({ ...env, OPENAI_QA_MODEL: " gpt-6-luna " })).toEqual({
      apiKey: API_KEY,
      model: "gpt-6-luna",
    });
  });
});

describe("createOpenAiQaModel request flow", () => {
  it("never calls the provider when Q&A is disabled or not configured", async () => {
    const { fetcher } = provider();

    expect(await codeOf(createOpenAiQaModel({ fetcher, env: {} })(request))).toBe("QA_DISABLED");
    expect(
      await codeOf(createOpenAiQaModel({ fetcher, env: { ...env, OPENAI_API_KEY: "" } })(request)),
    ).toBe("API_KEY_NOT_CONFIGURED");
    expect(
      await codeOf(createOpenAiQaModel({ fetcher, env: { ...env, OPENAI_QA_MODEL: "" } })(request)),
    ).toBe("MODEL_NOT_CONFIGURED");
    expect(fetcher).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
  });

  it("counts tokens first, then generates, with the same model, instructions, input, and format", async () => {
    const { fetcher } = provider();
    const timeoutSpy = jest.spyOn(AbortSignal, "timeout");

  it.each<[string, unknown]>([
    ["a missing count", { object: "response.input_tokens" }],
    ["a negative count", countBody(-1)],
    ["a fractional count", countBody(14_000.5)],
    ["a string count", countBody("14000")],
    ["a null count", { object: "response.input_tokens", input_tokens: null }],
    ["an unsafe integer", countBody(Number.MAX_SAFE_INTEGER + 2)],
    ["the wrong object type", { object: "response", input_tokens: 10 }],
    ["a non-object body", [10]],
  ])("fails closed with zero generation calls for %s", async (_label, body) => {
    const { fetcher, generationCalls } = provider({ count: () => jsonResponse(body) });

    const error = await rejectionOf(createOpenAiQaModel({ fetcher, env })(request));
    expect(error).toMatchObject({ name: "QaBudgetError", code: "BUDGET_CHECK_FAILED" });
    expect(generationCalls()).toHaveLength(0);
  });

  it.each<[string, () => Reply, string]>([
    ["HTTP 429", () => new Response("{}", { status: 429 }), "MODEL_RATE_LIMITED"],
    ["HTTP 401", () => new Response("{}", { status: 401 }), "MODEL_AUTH"],
    ["HTTP 400", () => new Response("{}", { status: 400 }), "MODEL_REJECTED"],
    ["HTTP 500", () => new Response("{}", { status: 500 }), "MODEL_UNAVAILABLE"],
    ["a timeout", () => new DOMException("timed out", "TimeoutError"), "MODEL_TIMEOUT"],
    ["a network failure", () => new TypeError("fetch failed SECRET_MARKER"), "MODEL_UNAVAILABLE"],
    ["a non-JSON body", () => new Response("<html>gateway</html>"), "MODEL_FAILED"],
  ])(
    "makes zero generation calls when the count request fails with %s",
    async (_label, count, code) => {
      const { fetcher, generationCalls } = provider({ count });

      const error = await rejectionOf(createOpenAiQaModel({ fetcher, env })(request));
      expect(error).toMatchObject({ name: "QaModelError", code });
      expect(generationCalls()).toHaveLength(0);
    },
  );

  it("names the failing stage in the log-only detail", async () => {
    const { fetcher } = provider({ count: () => new Response("{}", { status: 503 }) });

    const error = await rejectionOf(createOpenAiQaModel({ fetcher, env })(request));
    expect((error as QaModelError).detail).toBe("OpenAI token count request failed with HTTP 503.");
  });

  it("makes no provider calls at all for a payload over the size ceiling", async () => {
    const { fetcher } = provider();
    const huge = { ...request, input: "x".repeat(MAX_COUNTABLE_REQUEST_BYTES) };

    const error = await rejectionOf(createOpenAiQaModel({ fetcher, env })(huge));
    expect(error).toMatchObject({ name: "QaPromptError", code: "REQUEST_TOO_LARGE" });
    expect(fetcher).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
  });
});
```

```
  readonly strategy: "all-persisted-reviews";

const TARGET_ID = "aaaaaaaa-aaaa-4aaa-8aaa-aaaaaaaaaaa1";
const OTHER_TARGET_ID = "bbbbbbbb-bbbb-4bbb-8bbb-bbbbbbbbbbbb";
const SOURCE_URL_MARKER = "https://www.google.com/maps/place/SOURCE_URL_MARKER";

function review(index: number, text: string, targetId = TARGET_ID): S2010ReviewEvidence {
  return {
    id: `00000000-0000-4000-8000-${String(index).padStart(12, "0")}`,
    analysisTargetId: targetId,
    rating: 4,
    text,
    reviewDate: "2026-09-01T18:00:00.000Z",
  };
}

/** Builds a context the way S2-010 does, including its evidenceJson/evidenceBytes fields. */
function makeContext(reviews: S2010ReviewEvidence[]): S2010ReviewDatasetContext {
  const target = {
    id: TARGET_ID,
    name: "Restaurant A",
    platform: "google_maps" as const,
    sourceUrl: SOURCE_URL_MARKER,

      expect(error).toMatchObject({ name: "QaPromptError", code: "REVIEW_TARGET_MISMATCH" });
      expect((error as Error).message).not.toContain("TARGET_B_MARKER");
      expect(deps.invokeModel).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
    });
  });

  it("passes S2-010's largest allowed evidence plus a 2,000-character question through the size ceiling", async () => {
    // Grow review text until the S2-010 evidence measurement is as close to its cap as possible.
    const contextWithTextLength = (length: number) =>
      makeContext(
        Array.from({ length: S2010_MAX_CONTEXT_REVIEWS }, (_, i) =>
          review(i, "The service was friendly. ".repeat(50).slice(0, length)),
        ),
      );
    let textLength = 1;
    while (
      contextWithTextLength(textLength + 1).evidenceBytes <= S2010_MAX_CONTEXT_EVIDENCE_BYTES
    ) {
      textLength += 1;
    }
    const maxContext = contextWithTextLength(textLength);
    expect(maxContext.evidenceBytes).toBeLessThanOrEqual(S2010_MAX_CONTEXT_EVIDENCE_BYTES);
    expect(maxContext.evidenceBytes).toBeGreaterThan(S2010_MAX_CONTEXT_EVIDENCE_BYTES - 200);
    const { deps } = setup(maxContext);

    await runGroundedQaRequest(TARGET_ID, "q".repeat(2_000), deps);

    // The pre-network size ceiling must never block S2-010's largest allowed dataset; the exact
    // token count then comes from OpenAI's input_tokens endpoint (tested in the adapter suite).
    const request = deps.invokeModel.mock.calls[0][0];
    expect(() =>
      assertCountableSize(buildInputTokenCountBody(buildOpenAiRequestBody(request, "gpt-6-luna"))),
    ).not.toThrow();
  });
});
```

````
}

/**
 * Request-size budget. QA_MAX_REQUEST_TOKENS is the single configurable cap on one model request:
 * system prompt + review evidence + question + structured-output schema + provider overhead +
 * reserved output must fit inside it. S2-010's 100-review / 32 KiB evidence limit is one part of
 * this budget and is enforced there first. The full budget is enforced with model-compatible
 * token counting immediately before the provider call (`qa/token-budget.ts`), because the exact
 * count depends on the configured model. These are application budgets for cost and latency, not
 * the model's context window.
 */
export const QA_MAX_REQUEST_TOKENS = 16_000;

/** Output tokens reserved for the reply. S2-011 must send this as the provider's output limit. */
export const QA_RESERVED_OUTPUT_TOKENS = 1_500;


`answerQuestion(targetId, question, { buildReviewDatasetContext, callModel })`
(`src/services/qa/answer-question.ts`):

1. **Context (S2-010):** `buildReviewDatasetContext(targetId)` loads the signed-in owner's reviews
   for the active target, or throws.
2. **Request (S2-013):** `buildQaModelRequest` builds the system instructions, the review dataset,
   and the question, and checks the request-size budget, or throws `QaPromptError`.
3. **Model call (S2-011):** `callModel(request)`. In production this is `createOpenAiQaModel()`;
   tests pass a deterministic fake.
4. **Reply validation (S2-011):** `parseQaModelReply` checks the reply, or throws
   `QaResponseError`.

A failure at any step throws, and nothing unvalidated is returned. Steps 1 and 2 run before any
model call.

**Integration entry point.** The Q&A route calls `answerQuestion()`. It is built on
`runGroundedQaRequest()` (`qa-request.ts`, S2-013), which runs steps 1–3 and stops before the model
call if the context or request builder throws. Do not call `runGroundedQaRequest()` directly from a
route: it does not validate the reply.

| Error                                       | From          | Meaning                                                                    |
| ------------------------------------------- | ------------- | -------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `ReviewContextError`, `TargetNotFoundError` | S2-010        | Not signed in, foreign or missing target, no data                          |
| `QaPromptError`                             | S2-013        | Empty question, mismatched data; `REQUEST_TOO_LARGE` from the budget check |
| `QaBudgetError`                             | Budget check  | OpenAI token-count response was invalid (fails closed)                     |
| `QaModelError`                              | OpenAI client | Disabled, not configured, provider failure                                 |
| `QaResponseError`                           | Validation    | Malformed or ungrounded reply                                              |

Every error message is user-safe. None includes review text, model output, provider bodies, or
the API key.

## Reply validation (`qa-response.ts`)

- **HTTP errors:** 429 → `MODEL_RATE_LIMITED`; 401 / 403 → `MODEL_AUTH`; other 4xx (except 408) →
  `MODEL_REJECTED`; 408 / 5xx / network → `MODEL_UNAVAILABLE`; timeout → `MODEL_TIMEOUT`.

### Request budget (`token-budget.ts`)

Each question makes up to two OpenAI calls, in this order:

1. **Size ceiling (no network).** If the serialized count request exceeds
   `MAX_COUNTABLE_REQUEST_BYTES` (128 KiB), the result is `REQUEST_TOO_LARGE` and nothing is sent to
   OpenAI. Real requests are far below this (S2-010 caps evidence at 32 KiB); it only stops
   absurd payloads from leaving the server.
2. **Token count:** `POST /v1/responses/input_tokens` with the same `model`, `instructions`,
   `input`, and `text.format` (structured-output schema) as the generation request.
3. **Budget check:** generation is allowed only if
   `input_tokens + QA_RESERVED_OUTPUT_TOKENS (1,500) <= QA_MAX_REQUEST_TOKENS (16,000)`, that is,
   at most 14,500 input tokens.
4. **Generation:** `POST /v1/responses`, still with `max_output_tokens: 1500`.

**Why OpenAI's count:** the official token-counting guide says the endpoint returns "the exact
count the model will receive" and "includes formatting tokens used to represent request
structure, such as message roles and boundaries". That removes the need for a guessed overhead
allowance and works for any configured model, with no local tokenizer dependency.

**Fail closed.** No generation call is made if:

- the count request fails (HTTP error, timeout, or network failure → the usual `QaModelError`
  codes, with a log-only `detail` naming the "token count" stage);
- the count response is not exactly `{"object": "response.input_tokens", "input_tokens": <safe
non-negative integer>}` (`QaBudgetError("BUDGET_CHECK_FAILED")`);
- the count is over budget (`QaPromptError("REQUEST_TOO_LARGE")`).

**Tests (mocked endpoints, no real calls):** a count of 14,500 allows generation, and 14,501
blocks it. Every invalid count shape and every count-request failure makes zero generation calls,
and a payload over the size ceiling makes no calls at all. Both requests carry the same model,
instructions, input, and format. S2-010's largest allowed evidence plus a 2,000-character question
passes the size ceiling.

**Limitations**

- **Not yet verified live.** These fields were checked against the docs: the count body omits
  `max_output_tokens` and `store`, because they don't affect input size and aren't documented for
  that endpoint. The docs did not confirm that the top-level `text.format` is accepted by the count
  endpoint. If it is rejected, counting fails closed. The smoke test must confirm that the endpoint
  accepts this body and that `input_tokens` matches the generation response's `usage.input_tokens`.
- **Billing and rate limits** of the count endpoint were not stated in the docs read. Each
  question makes one extra API call.
- **Reasoning models:** reasoning tokens count against `max_output_tokens`. A reply that runs out
  ends as `RESPONSE_INCOMPLETE`; it never exceeds the budget.
- **Only the OpenAI adapter enforces the budget.** A fake `callModel` in tests does not.

### Configuration

| Variable          | Required              | Notes                                                   |
| ----------------- | --------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------- |
| `QA_ENABLED`      | Yes, exactly `"true"` | Any other value disables Q&A before any network request |
  buildReviewDatasetContext: async () => context,
  // Option A: a fake model that returns a fixed reply.
  callModel: async () => ({
    text: JSON.stringify({ outcome: "answered", answer: "Slow service.", citedReviewIds: ["r1"] }),
  }),
  // Option B: the real adapter with a fake fetch (inspect the outgoing request body):
  // callModel: createOpenAiQaModel({ fetcher: fakeFetch, env: { QA_ENABLED: "true",
  //   OPENAI_API_KEY: "test", OPENAI_QA_MODEL: "test-model" } }),
});
```

Run the existing suites with `npx jest tests/unit/services/qa`. The controlled live smoke test
(real key, synthetic data, spending limit approved) is a separate, manual step that the team
approves first.

## Not in this change

- **Q&A Route Handler and UI (S2-009):** the route must use `requireApiUser()`, apply the
  question length limit, pass S2-010's `buildReviewDatasetContext` and `createOpenAiQaModel()`
  into `answerQuestion`, and map each error class to a response.
- **S2-010 wiring:** `buildReviewDatasetContext` is injected because PR #78 is not merged yet.
- **Live evaluation:** the S2-003 harness and the S2-004 evaluation set measure real model
  behavior. Q&A stays disabled (`QA_ENABLED`) until that run and team review.
- **Model choice:** `OPENAI_QA_MODEL` is a team decision. Neel suggested `gpt-6-luna` for the
  first controlled evaluation, if the API project supports it.
````

```
## S2-011 review Q&A

`qa/answer-question.ts` runs the Q&A pipeline: S2-010 review context → S2-013 model request →
model call → reply validation (`qa/qa-response.ts`). Before the model call, the shared service
boundary (`qa/qa-request.ts`) rejects a non-string question, a question over 2,000 characters, or a
serialized request over 128 KiB with `QaInputValidationError`; the OpenAI adapter enforces the token
budget. Both the context builder and the model caller
are injected, so tests run without a database or a paid model call. The production model caller is
```

### Tool result — 2026-11-14T17:55:16.058Z

```
`answerQuestion(targetId, question, { buildReviewDatasetContext, callModel })`
(`src/services/qa/answer-question.ts`):

1. **Question validation (service boundary):** the question must be a string of at most 2,000
   characters after trimming, or `QaInputValidationError` is thrown. No data is loaded first.
2. **Context (S2-010):** `buildReviewDatasetContext(targetId)` loads the signed-in owner's reviews
   for the active target, or throws.
3. **Request (S2-013):** `buildQaModelRequest` builds the system instructions, the review dataset,
   and the question. It rejects an empty question, an empty dataset, and reviews from another
   target (`QaPromptError`). It does **not** check the request size or token budget.
4. **Request size (service boundary):** `JSON.stringify(request)` must be at most 128 KiB, or
   `QaInputValidationError` is thrown.
5. **Model call (S2-011):** `callModel(request)`. In production this is `createOpenAiQaModel()`,
   which enforces the token budget before generating; tests pass a deterministic fake.
6. **Reply validation (S2-011):** `parseQaModelReply` checks the reply, or throws
   `QaResponseError`.

A failure at any step throws, and nothing unvalidated is returned. Steps 1–4 run before the
injected model caller.

**Integration entry point.** The Q&A route calls `answerQuestion()`. It is built on
`runGroundedQaRequest()` (`qa-request.ts`), the shared service boundary, which runs steps 1–5 and
stops before the model call if any earlier step throws. Do not call `runGroundedQaRequest()`
directly from a route: it does not validate the reply.

### Where each limit is enforced

| Limit                                                  | Enforced in                                         | When                         | Error                                               |
| ------------------------------------------------------ | --------------------------------------------------- | ---------------------------- | --------------------------------------------------- |
| Question is a string                                   | Service boundary (`runGroundedQaRequest`)           | Before the context is loaded | `QaInputValidationError` `QA_QUESTION_INVALID_TYPE` |
| Question ≤ 2,000 characters after trimming             | Service boundary (same limit as the S2-009 route)   | Before the context is loaded | `QaInputValidationError` `QA_QUESTION_TOO_LONG`     |
| Question not blank                                     | `buildQaModelRequest` (S2-013)                      | While building the request   | `QaPromptError` `EMPTY_QUESTION`                    |
| Evidence ≤ 100 reviews and 32 KiB                      | `buildReviewDatasetContext` (S2-010)                | While loading the context    | `ReviewContextError` `REVIEW_CONTEXT_TOO_LARGE`     |
| Serialized request ≤ 128 KiB (`QA_MAX_REQUEST_BYTES`)  | Service boundary                                    | After the request is built   | `QaInputValidationError` `QA_REQUEST_TOO_LARGE`     |
| Serialized count body ≤ 128 KiB                        | OpenAI adapter (`token-budget.ts`)                  | Before any network call      | `QaPromptError` `REQUEST_TOO_LARGE`                 |
| Input ≤ 14,500 tokens (16,000 − 1,500 reserved output) | OpenAI adapter, using OpenAI's `input_tokens` count | Before the generation call   | `QaPromptError` `REQUEST_TOO_LARGE`                 |
| Token count is valid                                   | OpenAI adapter                                      | Before the generation call   | `QaBudgetError` `BUDGET_CHECK_FAILED`               |
| Output ≤ 1,500 tokens                                  | OpenAI (`max_output_tokens`)                        | During generation            | `QaModelError` `RESPONSE_INCOMPLETE`                |
| Provider response ≤ 256 KiB                            | OpenAI adapter                                      | While reading the response   | `QaModelError` `MODEL_FAILED`                       |

The service-boundary checks apply to every caller (the route, the S2-003 harness, scripts) and to
any injected model caller, including test fakes. Token counting needs the configured model and
OpenAI's endpoint, so it stays in the adapter.

| Error                                       | From             | Meaning                                                                              |
| ------------------------------------------- | ---------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| `QaInputValidationError`                    | Service boundary | Question not a string or too long; serialized request too large                      |
| `ReviewContextError`, `TargetNotFoundError` | S2-010           | Not signed in, foreign or missing target, no data                                    |
| `QaPromptError`                             | S2-013 / adapter | Empty question, mismatched data; `REQUEST_TOO_LARGE` from the adapter's token budget |
| `QaBudgetError`                             | Budget check     | OpenAI token-count response was invalid (fails closed)                               |
| `QaModelError`                              | OpenAI client    | Disabled, not configured, provider failure                                           |
| `QaResponseError`                           | Validation       | Malformed or ungrounded reply                                                        |
```

```
}

/**
 * Service-boundary limits, checked in `runGroundedQaRequest` before the injected model caller runs.
 * They apply to every caller (route, harness, scripts), not only the public route, and do not
 * depend on the provider. Provider token counting stays in the OpenAI adapter
 * (`openai-qa-client.ts` / `token-budget.ts`).
 */
/** Matches the S2-009 route's question limit (2,000 characters after trimming). */
export const QA_MAX_QUESTION_CHARACTERS = 2_000;
/** Largest UTF-8 size of the serialized model request (`JSON.stringify(request)`): 128 KiB. */
export const QA_MAX_REQUEST_BYTES = 131_072;

export type QaInputValidationErrorCode =
  "QA_QUESTION_INVALID_TYPE" | "QA_QUESTION_TOO_LONG" | "QA_REQUEST_TOO_LARGE";

const INPUT_MESSAGES: Record<QaInputValidationErrorCode, string> = {
  QA_QUESTION_INVALID_TYPE: "Enter a question about the reviews.",
  QA_QUESTION_TOO_LONG: `Questions can be up to ${QA_MAX_QUESTION_CHARACTERS.toLocaleString("en-US")} characters.`,
  QA_REQUEST_TOO_LARGE:
    "This question is too long to analyze with these reviews. Try a shorter question.",
};

/**
 * Input rejected at the shared service boundary, before any model call. The code is stable, so
 * routes and the S2-003 harness can match on it. The message is user-safe; `detail` is for server
 * logs and holds only sizes and types, never question or review text.
 */
export class QaInputValidationError extends Error {
  constructor(
    readonly code: QaInputValidationErrorCode,
    readonly detail: string,
  ) {
    super(INPUT_MESSAGES[code]);
    this.name = "QaInputValidationError";
  }
}

/** Throws QaInputValidationError unless `question` is a string within the length limit. */
export function assertValidQuestion(question: unknown): asserts question is string {
  if (typeof question !== "string") {
    throw new QaInputValidationError(
      "QA_QUESTION_INVALID_TYPE",
      `Question must be a string; received ${question === null ? "null" : typeof question}.`,
    );
  }
  const length = question.trim().length;
  if (length > QA_MAX_QUESTION_CHARACTERS) {
    throw new QaInputValidationError(
      "QA_QUESTION_TOO_LONG",
      `Question is ${length} characters after trimming; the limit is ${QA_MAX_QUESTION_CHARACTERS}.`,
    );
  }
}

/** Throws QaInputValidationError if the serialized request exceeds QA_MAX_REQUEST_BYTES. */
export function assertRequestWithinSizeLimit(request: QaModelRequest): void {
  const bytes = Buffer.byteLength(JSON.stringify(request), "utf8");
  if (bytes > QA_MAX_REQUEST_BYTES) {
    throw new QaInputValidationError(
      "QA_REQUEST_TOO_LARGE",
      `Serialized model request is ${bytes} bytes; the limit is ${QA_MAX_REQUEST_BYTES}.`,
    );
  }
}

/** Builds the authorized context for a target, or throws. In production: S2-010's builder. */
export type QaReviewContextBuilder = (targetId: unknown) => Promise<QaReviewContext>;

/** The single model boundary. S2-011 supplies the provider call; S2-003 supplies a mock. */
export type QaModelInvoker<T> = (request: QaModelRequest) => Promise<T>;

export interface GroundedQaDependencies<T> {
```
